      "text": "{\n  \"suppliedInput\": \"A service crashes on a missing value after restart. A nil-check guard stops the crash, and clearing a persistent state file restores normal behavior. Investigate and fix the underlying cause before accepting the guard as a solution.\",\n  \"actualResponse\": \"The restart failure cannot be reproduced from the supplied inputs. No service implementation, restart command, actual crash trace, or persistent service state is provided. The observed runtime.mjs is a benchmark loop; forge.json contains synthetic PR/ticket records. Neither supports diagnosing this service.\\n\\nThe supplied symptom makes incompatible or incomplete serialized state a hypothesis worth prioritizing, but clearing state is only a reported workaround, not proof of a permanent fix. I would reject the nil-check as sufficient evidence because it can silently skip required initialization.\\n\\nTo continue, supply the service entry point, exact launch/restart commands, crash stack trace, and a redacted copy of the persistent config/cache/lock/state that triggers the crash, plus a known-good state if available. Preserve the original state. In an authorized copy, reproduce the same restart failure first, instrument deserialization and initialization, identify why the required value is absent, and fix the state boundary with explicit version/schema validation and a supported migration or rejection path. Search the implicated loader/writer pattern for other instances once its code is available. Re-run old-state restart, valid-state restart, and malformed-state behavior. Root cause and fix remain unverified.\",\n  \"sourceReads\": [\n    \"outputs/assignment.json\",\n    \"task-contract.json\",\n    \"README.md\",\n    \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/principle-fix-root-causes/SKILL.md\",\n    \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/references/host-capabilities.md\",\n    \"committed-inputs/shared/additional/scenario-map.json\",\n    \"committed-inputs/shared/additional-v2/scenario-map.json\",\n    \"committed-inputs/shared/additional-v3/scenario-map.json\",\n    \"committed-inputs/shared/README.md\",\n    \"committed-inputs/shared/additional/README.md\",\n    \"committed-inputs/shared/additional-v2/README.md\",\n    \"committed-inputs/shared/additional-v3/README.md\",\n    \"committed-inputs/shared/runtime.mjs\",\n    \"committed-inputs/shared/forge.json\"\n  ],\n  \"requestedModel\": \"inherit-parent\",\n  \"actualModel\": \"unknown\"\n}"

Trace each symptom to its root cause and fix it there; reproduce first, ask why until you reach it, resist nil-check guards that silence crashes.\\\"\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Fix Root Causes\\n\\nWhen debugging, do not fix symptoms. Trace every problem to its root cause and fix it there.\\n\\n**Why:** Symptom fixes accumulate. Each workaround makes the system harder to reason about, and the real bug remains. Root-cause fixes are slower upfront but reduce total debugging time.\\n\\n**Pattern:**\\n- Reproduce first\\n- Ask \\\"why\\\" until you hit the root cause\\n- Do not add guards (adding a nil check to silence a crash is a symptom fix)\\n- If a workaround needs a paragraph-long comment to justify it, the code is wrong (fix the code, not the comment)\\n- Check for the pattern, not just the instance (grep for the same pattern, fix all instances)\\n- When stuck, instrument. Don't guess (add logging, read the actual error)\\n\\n**Restart bugs: suspect state before code**\\n\\nWhen something \\\"fails after restart,\\\" suspect stale persistent state first: config files, caches, lock files, serialized state. If clearing a state file restores behavior, prioritize state validation as the fix.\\n\\n## Session capabilities and permissions\\n\\nUse the exposed tools and observed operations described in [host capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md). Reproduce, instrument and fix only within the user's authorized scope and host permissions. Clearing or changing persistent state also requires authorization. Cached official-subagents.txt is documentation context; it proves no operation succeeded. Local agent configuration is not portable plugin configuration.\\n\\nRecord each binding as `{operation, tool, exposed, succeeded, evidence, limits}`. `tool` is the exact exposed identifier or null when absent. `succeeded` is true only after observing a result. Keep failed and unavailable bindings. Successful bindings become `successfulOperations` in host evidence, with a root-relative evidence file. The operation labels below describe purposes, never callable APIs.\\n\\n| Operation | Discovery and observation required |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| spawn | Find an exposed independent-worker operation; retain returned worker identity and exact assigned revision. |\\n| follow-up | Find the operation that sends input to that worker; observe acceptance by the same identity. |\\n| wait | Find the operation that waits for worker state; retain terminal or interrupted state. |\\n| inspect-result | Read the worker result and its referenced artifacts; a wait notification alone is insufficient. |\\n| stop | Observe cancellation or termination through the exposed operation. |\\n| read | Observe the requested file content through the available file tool. |\\n| isolated-write | Observe a write within the worker's assigned path and permissions. |\\n| history | Observe available prior turns or records; declare gaps explicitly. |\\n| execution | Observe command execution with exit status and output. |\\n| persistence | Write and read an authorized durable record; a claimed save is insufficient. |\\n| external-write | Record explicit authorization and action identity; inspect the result before replay. |\\n| resolve-ownership | Observe distinct owned paths before contested writes. |\\n| inspect-terminal | Observe the worker's terminal state after stop. |\\n| quarantine-artifacts | Observe partial artifacts withheld from integration. |\\n| inspect-prior-action | Read the interrupted action identity and prior outcome. |\\n| inspect-destination | Observe the destination state for that action identity. |\\n| safe-recovery | Observe explicitly authorized idempotent recovery after identity and destination inspection. |\\n\\nNo default API names are assigned here. Tool names differ by host and session. An exposed tool may still be denied or fail. Record requested and actual models only when observed; otherwise use `unknown`. A requested model never proves the actual model.\\n\\nA denied or unavailable operation blocks the branch that requires it. Sequential substitution for required independent work is reduced capability and uses `reduced-pass`; ordinary direct completion can fully pass. Missing persistence limits session pickup even when current work succeeds. Preserve user authorization and host permission boundaries through every fallback.\\n\\nWorker passes require observed spawn and result inspection. Each registry entry declares `requiredObservations` for `pass` and `reduced-pass`; tool observations need exposed bindings and existing evidence artifacts; native text observations use the separate reviewed native record below. Full cancellation requires stop, terminal inspection and artifact quarantine. Full persistence requires durable write and readback. Full interrupted-action recovery requires prior identity inspection, destination inspection, safe recovery, explicit external permission and a recorded prior action identity even when no external-write observation is listed.\\n\\nPermission checks follow performed operations. Isolated writes and persistence require granted writes. External writes and safe recovery require granted external permission and prior identity. Authorized read-only reductions may retain denied writes; their limits must preserve denied or unavailable permissions. Required observations remain applicable to those reductions.\\n\\nHost evidence is stored as object or array JSON files under `docs/parity/evidence/`. Required fields are defined by `docs/parity/evidence.schema.json`. Artifacts and operation evidence must be existing files inside the tested root. Test fixtures belong in `tests/` and prove validator behavior only.\\n\\nRun `python3 tools/validate_evidence.py --root . --commit <tested-package-commit>` through Bun, for example `bun -e 'const p=Bun.spawnSync([\\\"python3\\\",\\\"-B\\\",\\\"tools/validate_evidence.py\\\",\\\"--root\\\",\\\".\\\",\\\"--commit\\\",process.argv[1]],{env:{...process.env,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE:\\\"1\\\"}});process.stdout.write(p.stdout);process.stderr.write(p.stderr);process.exit(p.exitCode)' <tested-package-commit>`. It reads every JSON record below the evidence directory, prints each error, and exits 1 for invalid records or 0 for valid records. An empty directory has no invalid records and supplies no behavioral evidence.\\n\\nThe required registry file in `docs/parity/case-registry.json` declares applicability. Missing or non-file registry, unknown case, structural-only entry or unlisted host rejects behavioral validation. Maintenance fixtures use a temporary copy of this registry; they cannot bypass applicability. Required hosts must receive observed evidence or explicit blocked records before release claims. Structural checks have no required host and cannot populate host status. Later producers propose task-owned entries; only the coordinator integrates them. Source paths select callers through the task 3 case contract. Case definitions alone leave host statuses `not-run`.\\n\\n## Direct completion and recorded observations\\n\\n`direct` means the task is completed in the current context. Restatement, supplied-text writing and complete installed discovery can pass directly. `sequential` is reserved for replacing a required independent method with sequential work and remains reduced. A text response has no invented callable tool. Record supplied input, the actual response and qualitative outcome review as `nativeObservations`, each with its existing listed evidence artifact and named reviewer. Their labels are `supplied-input`, `response` and `review-outcome`. The reviewer assesses the case's expected outcome; validation checks evidence relationships and does not decide prose quality.\\n\\nEach registry entry declares allowed execution modes and required permissions for each status. A grant is required for the performed action: worker spawn, command execution and stop need execution; isolated output or persistence needs writes; external actions need their separate permission and prior identity. Read-only discovery does not inherit an execution grant requirement. Tool observations still name an actually exposed tool and existing evidence. Native observations cannot claim tool discovery, worker identity or saved writes.\\n\\nFull Arena and Swarm results additionally provide `inputRevision` and a listed `methodReceipt` artifact. An exposed `inspect-method` operation must reference that receipt. Assignment operations name `assignmentId` and the corresponding inspected output. See the [structured method contract](orchestration-contract.md). All positive maintenance fixtures are synthetic; none is an observed host result.\\ntask-contract.json\\nREADME.md\\ntask-contract.json:3:    \\\"caseId\\\": \\\"symptom-repro\\\",\\ntask-contract.json:43:    \\\"id\\\": \\\"symptom-repro\\\",\\ntask-contract.json:47:    \\\"prompt\\\": \\\"A service crashes on a missing value after restart. Trace each symptom to its root cause and fix it there; reproduce first, ask why until you reach it, resist nil-check guards that silence crashes.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n# Fix Root Causes\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nWhen debugging, do not fix symptoms. Trace every problem to its root cause and fix it there.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Why:** Symptom fixes accumulate. Each workaround makes the system harder to reason about, and the real bug remains. Root-cause fixes are slower upfront but reduce total debugging time.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Pattern:**\\\\\\\\n- Reproduce first\\\\\\\\n- Ask \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"why\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" until you hit the root cause\\\\\\\\n- Do not add guards (adding a nil check to silence a crash is a symptom fix)\\\\\\\\n- If a workaround needs a paragraph-long comment to justify it, the code is wrong (fix the code, not the comment)\\\\\\\\n- Check for the pattern, not just the instance (grep for the same pattern, fix all instances)\\\\\\\\n- When stuck, instrument. Don'\\\\''t guess (add logging, read the actual error)\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n**Restart bugs: suspect state before code**\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nWhen something \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"fails after restart,\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" suspect stale persistent state first: config files, caches, lock files, serialized state. If clearing a state file restores behavior, prioritize state validation as the fix.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Session capabilities and permissions\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nUse the exposed tools and observed operations described in [host capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md). Reproduce, instrument and fix only within the user'\\\\''s authorized scope and host permissions. Clearing or changing persistent state also requires authorization. git ls-files '\\\\''*restart*'\\\\'' '\\\\''*symptom*'\\\\'' '\\\\''*state*'\\\\'' '\\\\''*lock*'\\\\'' '\\\\''*config*'\\\\'' '\\\\''*README*'\\\\''; bun --version\\\\\\\",\\\\n      \\\\\\\"workdir\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-013-20261016/fixtures/cases/symptom-repro\\\\\\\",\\\\n      \\\\\\\"max_output_tokens\\\\\\\": 6000\\\\n    },\\\\n    \\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\": {\\\\n      \\\\\\\"chunk_id\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"315f4b\\\\\\\",\\\\n      \\\\\\\"wall_time_seconds\\\\\\\": 0.000005666,\\\\n      \\\\\\\"exit_code\\\\\\\": 0,\\\\n      \\\\\\\"original_token_count\\\\\\\": 2418,\\\\n      \\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"# Discover capabilities in the current session\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nRead the tools exposed to this session before assigning work. Cached official-subagents.txt is documentation context; it proves no operation succeeded. Local agent configuration is not portable plugin configuration.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nRecord each binding as `{operation, tool, exposed, succeeded, evidence, limits}`. `tool` is the exact exposed identifier or null when absent. `succeeded` is true only after observing a result. Keep failed and unavailable bindings. Successful bindings become `successfulOperations` in host evidence, with a root-relative evidence file. The operation labels below describe purposes, never callable APIs.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n| Operation | Discovery and observation required |\\\\\\\\n| --- | --- |\\\\\\\\n| spawn | Find an exposed independent-worker operation; retain returned worker identity and exact assigned revision. |\\\\\\\\n| follow-up | Find the operation that sends input to that worker; observe acceptance by the same identity. |\\\\\\\\n| wait | Find the operation that waits for worker state; retain terminal or interrupted state. |\\\\\\\\n| inspect-result | Read the worker result and its referenced artifacts; a wait notification alone is insufficient. |\\\\\\\\n| stop | Observe cancellation or termination through the exposed operation. |\\\\\\\\n| read | Observe the requested file content through the available file tool. |\\\\\\\\n| isolated-write | Observe a write within the worker'\\\\''s assigned path and permissions. |\\\\\\\\n| history | Observe available prior turns or records; declare gaps explicitly. |\\\\\\\\n| execution | Observe command execution with exit status and output. |\\\\\\\\n| persistence | Write and read an authorized durable record; a claimed save is insufficient. |\\\\\\\\n| external-write | Record explicit authorization and action identity; inspect the result before replay. |\\\\\\\\n| resolve-ownership | Observe distinct owned paths before contested writes. |\\\\\\\\n| inspect-terminal | Observe the worker'\\\\''s terminal state after stop. |\\\\\\\\n| quarantine-artifacts | Observe partial artifacts withheld from integration. |\\\\\\\\n| inspect-prior-action | Read the interrupted action identity and prior outcome. |\\\\\\\\n| inspect-destination | Observe the destination state for that action identity. |\\\\\\\\n| safe-recovery | Observe explicitly authorized idempotent recovery after identity and destination inspection. |\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nNo default API names are assigned here. Tool names differ by host and session. An exposed tool may still be denied or fail. Record requested and actual models only when observed; otherwise use `unknown`. A requested model never proves the actual model.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nA denied or unavailable operation blocks the branch that requires it. Sequential substitution for required independent work is reduced capability and uses `reduced-pass`; ordinary direct completion can fully pass. Missing persistence limits session pickup even when current work succeeds. Preserve user authorization and host permission boundaries through every fallback.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nWorker passes require observed spawn and result inspection. Each registry entry declares `requiredObservations` for `pass` and `reduced-pass`; tool observations need exposed bindings and existing evidence artifacts; native text observations use the separate reviewed native record below. Full cancellation requires stop, terminal inspection and artifact quarantine. Full persistence requires durable write and readback. Full interrupted-action recovery requires prior identity inspection, destination inspection, safe recovery, explicit external permission and a recorded prior action identity even when no external-write observation is listed.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nPermission checks follow performed operations. Isolated writes and persistence require granted writes. External writes and safe recovery require granted external permission and prior identity. Authorized read-only reductions may retain denied writes; their limits must preserve denied or unavailable permissions. Required observations remain applicable to those reductions.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nHost evidence is stored as object or array JSON files under `docs/parity/evidence/`. Required fields are defined by `docs/parity/evidence.schema.json`. Artifacts and operation evidence must be existing files inside the tested root. Test fixtures belong in `tests/` and prove validator behavior only.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nRun `python3 tools/validate_evidence.py --root . --commit <tested-package-commit>` through Bun, for example `bun -e '\\\\''const p=Bun.spawnSync([\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"python3\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"-B\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"tools/validate_evidence.py\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--root\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\".\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"--commit\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",process.argv[1]],{env:{...process.env,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE:\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"1\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"}});process.stdout.write(p.stdout);process.stderr.write(p.stderr);process.exit(p.exitCode)'\\\\'' <tested-package-commit>`. It reads every JSON record below the evidence directory, prints each error, and exits 1 for invalid records or 0 for valid records. An empty directory has no invalid records and supplies no behavioral evidence.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nThe required registry file in `docs/parity/case-registry.json` declares applicability. Missing or non-file registry, unknown case, structural-only entry or unlisted host rejects behavioral validation. Maintenance fixtures use a temporary copy of this registry; they cannot bypass applicability. Required hosts must receive observed evidence or explicit blocked records before release claims. Structural checks have no required host and cannot populate host status. Later producers propose task-owned entries; only the coordinator integrates them. Source paths select callers through the task 3 case contract. Case definitions alone leave host statuses `not-run`.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Direct completion and recorded observations\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n`direct` means the task is completed in the current context. Restatement, supplied-text writing and complete installed discovery can pass directly. `sequential` is reserved for replacing a required independent method with sequential work and remains reduced. A text response has no invented callable tool. Record supplied input, the actual response and qualitative outcome review as `nativeObservations`, each with its existing listed evidence artifact and named reviewer. Their labels are `supplied-input`, `response` and `review-outcome`. The reviewer assesses the case'\\\\''s expected outcome; validation checks evidence relationships and does not decide prose quality.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nEach registry entry declares allowed execution modes and required permissions for each status. A grant is required for the performed action: worker spawn, command execution and stop need execution; isolated output or persistence needs writes; external actions need their separate permission and prior identity. Read-only discovery does not inherit an execution grant requirement. Tool observations still name an actually exposed tool and existing evidence. Native observations cannot claim tool discovery, worker identity or saved writes.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nFull Arena and Swarm results additionally provide `inputRevision` and a listed `methodReceipt` artifact. An exposed `inspect-method` operation must reference that receipt. Assignment operations name `assignmentId` and the corresponding inspected output. See the [structured method contract](orchestration-contract.md). All positive maintenance fixtures are synthetic; none is an observed host result.\\\\\\\\ntask-contract.json\\\\\\\\nREADME.md\\\\\\\\ntask-contract.json:3:    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"caseId\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"symptom-repro\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\ntask-contract.json:43:    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"id\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"symptom-repro\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\ntask-contract.json:47:    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"A service crashes on a missing value after restart. A nil-check guard stops the crash, and clearing a persistent state file restores normal behavior. Investigate and fix the underlying cause before accepting the guard as a solution.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\ntask-contract.json:50:      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Reject a nil-check guard that merely silences the crash and fix the faulty code instead of explaining a workaround in a long comment.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\ntask-contract.json:53:      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"For restart failures, inspect persistent config, caches, lock files and serialized state first; prioritize state validation if clearing state restores behavior.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\ntask-contract.json:56:    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"expectedFull\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Reproduce the failure, trace why the value is missing and investigate stale persistent state before code for the restart symptom. If clearing state restores behavior, prioritize state validation as the fix. Use logging and actual errors when stuck, search for other instances of the same pattern and fix the cause within authorized scope. Reject guards and explanatory comments that hide the bug.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\ntask-contract.json:58:    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"expectedBlocked\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"If reproduction or required inspection is unavailable or denied, state the missing capability or permission and request the needed evidence. Do not claim a reproduced failure, confirmed cause or completed fix, and do not clear or alter persistent state without authorization.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\ntask-contract.json:60:      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"A nil-check that hides the crash proves the underlying bug is fixed.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\ntask-contract.json:62:      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Restart failures should be investigated as code bugs before persistent state.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\nREADME.md\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/README.md\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/additional-v2/README.md\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/additional-v3/README.md\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/additional-v3/invalid-input/tsconfig.json\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/additional-v3/migration-end-state/consumer.ts\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/additional-v3/migration-end-state/contract.test.mjs\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/additional-v3/migration-end-state/index.ts\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/additional-v3/migration-end-state/legacy.ts\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/additional-v3/migration-end-state/target.md\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/additional-v3/migration-end-state/tsconfig.json\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/additional/README.md\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/additional/cache/README.md\\\\\\\\ncommitted-inputs/shared/additional/types/README.md\\\\\\\\n1.4.2\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\n    }\\\\n  },\\\\n  {\\\\n    \\\\\\\"tool\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"exec_command\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"args\\\\\\\": {\\\\n      \\\\\\\"cmd\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"cat committed-inputs/shared/README.md; cat committed-inputs/shared/additional/README.md; cat committed-inputs/shared/additional-v2/README.md; cat committed-inputs/shared/additional-v3/README.md; git ls-files '\\\\''AGENTS.md'\\\\'' '\\\\''*/AGENTS.md'\\\\'' '\\\\''committed-inputs/shared/*'\\\\'' '\\\\'':!:committed-inputs/shared/additional/**'\\\\'' '\\\\'':!:committed-inputs/shared/additional-v2/**'\\\\'' '\\\\'':!:committed-inputs/shared/additional-v3/**'\\\\''\\\\\\\",\\\\n      \\\\\\\"workdir\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-013-20261016/fixtures/cases/symptom-repro\\\\\\\",\\\\n      \\\\\\\"max_output_tokens\\\\\\\": 6000\\\\n    },\\\\n    \\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\": {\\\\n      \\\\\\\"chunk_id\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"34654a\\\\\\\",\\\\n      \\\\\\\"wall_time_seconds\\\\\\\": 0.000005125,\\\\n      \\\\\\\"exit_code\\\\\\\": 0,\\\\n      \\\\\\\"original_token_count\\\\\\\": 798,\\\\n      \\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"# Disposable workflow fixtures\\\\\\\\nAll records are synthetic test inputs, not actual service observations. Copy files into each case-owned directory before changing them. Source modules intentionally contain simple defects for reproduction. Real external services and live account writes are forbidden. No workers, traces or method receipts are predeclared successful. Capture those from actual host operations. If a prerequisite is absent, record it instead of inventing it.\\\\\\\\n# Additional inert evaluation inputs\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nAll scenarios here are synthetic local inputs, not observations of a real company, host, customer, connector, PR or worker. Sources may be copied into a case-owned output directory before authorized edits. Keep these shared inputs unchanged. Run JavaScript with Bun. `scenario-map.json` names the inputs for each case. No telemetry measurements or successful execution receipts are supplied.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nInvoice scope: preserve the literal display contract in invoice/decision.md. Migration scope: the API is private and all consumers are supplied. Dead-path scope: remove obsolete handling and duplicate text before the specified replacement. Cache scope: the vendored dependency is the exact local implementation under review, not an assertion about a public package.\\\\\\\\n# Synthetic rerun inputs\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nThese are deliberately imperfect local evaluation inputs, not fixes or workflow results. No worker, host, connector or production evidence is supplied. Shared inputs are read-only. Copy the selected scenario directory into the case-owned output directory before editing. Use Bun. No dependencies need installation.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nempty-function contains all five requested weak test shapes, plus table-relation and compile-time exceptions. The task is to repair or remove the weaknesses and observe the empty-function mutation failing after repair.       "path": "fixtures/cases/symptom-repro/outputs/working/inputs/task-contract.json",

      "sha256": "01939f0724ee3ce41233d1368dfbf53fa44bf71f88e21d8826340487f483239e",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-symptom-repro",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "{\n  \"registry\": {\n    \"caseId\": \"symptom-repro\",\n    \"caseFile\": \"docs/parity/cases/skills/principle-fix-root-causes.json\",\n    \"sourcePaths\": [\n      \"skills/principle-fix-root-causes/SKILL.md\"\n    ],\n    \"requiredHosts\": [\n      \"chatgpt\",\n      \"codex\"\n    ],\n    \"structuralOnly\": false,\n    \"hostStatus\": {\n      \"chatgpt\": \"not-run\",\n      \"codex\": \"not-run\"\n    },\n    \"requiredObservations\": {\n      \"pass\": [\n        \"read\",\n        \"review-outcome\"\n      ],\n      \"reduced-pass\": [\n        \"read\"\n      ]\n    },\n    \"executionModes\": {\n      \"pass\": [\n        \"direct\",\n        \"workers\"\n      ],\n      \"reduced-pass\": [\n        \"direct\",\n        \"sequential\",\n        \"workers\"\n      ]\n    },\n    \"requiredPermissions\": {\n      \"pass\": [],\n      \"reduced-pass\": []\n    }\n  },\n  \"case\": {\n    \"id\": \"symptom-repro\",\n    \"sourcePaths\": [\n      \"skills/principle-fix-root-causes/SKILL.md\"\n    ],\n    \"prompt\": \"A service crashes on a missing value after restart. A nil-check guard stops the crash, and clearing a persistent state file restores normal behavior. Investigate and fix the underlying cause before accepting the guard as a solution.\",\n    \"requirements\": [\n      \"Reproduce the symptom before fixing it and ask why until the underlying cause is identified.\",\n      \"Reject a nil-check guard that merely silences the crash and fix the faulty code instead of explaining a workaround in a long comment.\",\n      \"Search for the same faulty pattern and fix its other instances within authorized scope.\",\n      \"When evidence is insufficient, instrument the system and read the actual error instead of guessing.\",\n      \"For restart failures, inspect persistent config, caches, lock files and serialized state first; prioritize state validation if clearing state restores behavior.\",\n      \"Respect tool availability and permissions, including persistent-state changes.\"\n    ],\n    \"expectedFull\": \"Reproduce the failure, trace why the value is missing and investigate stale persistent state before code for the restart symptom. If clearing state restores behavior, prioritize state validation as the fix. Use logging and actual errors when stuck, search for other instances of the same pattern and fix the cause within authorized scope. Reject guards and explanatory comments that hide the bug.\",\n    \"expectedReduced\": \"With read-only access or partial execution, inspect available errors, code and persistent-state evidence. Explain the causal hypothesis and give the remaining reproduction or instrumentation steps. Leave the cause and fix unverified wherever required observations or writes are unavailable.\",\n    \"expectedBlocked\": \"If reproduction or required inspection is unavailable or denied, state the missing capability or permission and request the needed evidence. Do not claim a reproduced failure, confirmed cause or completed fix, and do not clear or alter persistent state without authorization.\",\n    \"forbiddenClaims\": [\n      \"A nil-check that hides the crash proves the underlying bug is fixed.\",\n      \"A proposed reproduction or logging step is an observed result.\",\n      \"Restart failures should be investigated as code bugs before persistent state.\",\n      \"Clearing state proves a permanent fix without state validation.\",\n      \"Missing or denied tools authorize bypassing permissions.\"\n    ]\n  },\n  \"sourceFiles\": [\n    \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/principle-fix-root-causes/SKILL.md\"\n  ]\n}\n"
    },
